HISTORY AND DEVELOPMENT                                                        歷史沿革
1962    I Lan Foods Industrial Co., Ltd. (“I Lan Foods”) was established       於1962年5月成立宜蘭食品工業股份有限
        in May 1962, which manufactured canned agricultural products           公司（「宜蘭食品」），該公司當時從事製造
        mainly for export.                                                     罐頭農產品並以出口外銷業務為主。
1983    In 1983, we collaborated with Iwatsuka Confectionery Co., Ltd.         於1983年，我們與日本領先的米果製造商
        (“ICCL”), one of the leading Japanese rice cracker producers, to       之一的岩塚製菓株式會社（「岩塚製菓」）合
        jointly develop the rice cracker market in Taiwan region. Since        作，攜手開拓台灣地區米果市場。自1983
        1983, we have been producing and marketing our products under          年起，我們開始生產及營銷「旺旺」品牌的
        the “Want Want” brand.                                                 產品。
1989    We introduced the “Want Want” brand in the Chinese mainland in         1989年「旺旺」品牌進入中國境內市場。
        1989.
1992    We ventured into the Chinese mainland and established our first        於1992年進軍中國境內並在湖南省設立首
        subsidiary in Hunan province in 1992. In the following years, we       間位於中國境內的附屬公司。此後數年，
        grew from a pure rice cracker company to a diversified food and        我們成功從一家單一米果公司發展為多元
        beverages company.                                                     化的食品及飲料公司。
1996    In May 1996, Want Want Holdings Ltd. (“WWHL”), our subsidiary,         我們的附屬公司旺旺控股有限公司（「旺旺
        was listed on the Main Board of Singapore Exchange Securities          控股」）於1996年5月在新加坡證券交易所
        Trading Limited (“SGX-ST”) and subsequently diversified into the       有限公司（「新交所」）主板上市。其後，更
        hospital, hotel and property businesses and other investments.         將業務作多元化發展至醫院、酒店及地產
                                                                               業務以及其他投資項目。
2007    WWHL delisted from SGX-ST in September 2007 and conducted              旺旺控股於2007年9月在新交所除牌。隨
        a group restructuring which involved (i) the incorporation of Want     後進行重組，包括(i)成立中國旺旺控股有
        Want China Holdings Limited (the “Company”) as the new holding         限公司（「本公司」）為其所有食品及飲料業
        company of our core operations related to the food and beverages       務的新投資控股公司；(ii)把醫院、酒店及
        businesses; (ii) the divestment and transfer of the hospital, hotel    地產業務以及其他投資項目剝離並於2007
        and property businesses and other investments to San Want              年12月31日轉移至San Want Holdings
        Holdings Limited (“San Want”) on 31 December 2007, and the             Limited（「神旺」），再透過向當時旺旺控股
        interests in San Want were distributed to the then shareholders of     股東派發神旺權益的實物股息而剝離。
        WWHL by way of dividend in specie.
2008    Our Company was listed on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong              於2008年3月26日，本公司正式在香港聯
        Limited (the “HK Stock Exchange”) on 26 March 2008 (the                合交易所有限公司（「香港聯交所」）上市
        “Listing”).                                                           （「上市」）。
2009-   Our Taiwan Depositary Receipts were listed on the Taiwan Stock         於2009年4月28日，我們的台灣存託憑證
2013    Exchange Corporation on 28 April 2009 and were voluntarily             在台灣證券交易所股份有限公司上市並於
        withdrawn from listing on 15 October 2013.                             2013年10月15日自願終止上市。
2020    In 2020, our Vietnam production base commenced construction            於2020年，越南生產基地開始動工，以
        to strengthen our business expansion and to cope with our future       強化集團在海外市場的業務拓展及未來佈
        development strategy in overseas markets.                              局。
2022    In 2022, our new factory in Tiền Giang province, Vietnam               於2022年，在越南前江市新設的工廠開始
        commenced production, representing the first step of Want Want         投入生產，這是我們旺旺為「世界夢」跨出

distribution and sale of rice crackers, dairy products and beverages, snack   飲料、休閒食品及其他產品之製造、分銷
foods and other products.                                                     及銷售。


KEY MARKETS                                                                   主要市場
Most of our operations are located in the Chinese mainland, which is one      我們的業務大部份位於中國境內（全球增
of the fastest growing economies in the world. We have an extensive           長速度最快經濟體之一）。我們在中國境
nationwide sales and distribution network throughout the Chinese              內的全國性銷售及分銷網絡龐大，我們也
mainland. We also export our products to other markets, including North       出口產品至其他市場：例如北美、東亞、
America, East Asia, South East Asia and Europe.                               東南亞及歐洲。

and 81 factories in the Chinese mainland and worked with over 10,000          422間營業所，35個生產基地及81間工
distributors.                                                                 廠，合作經銷商超過1萬名。

setting sail” to celebrate the 60th anniversary of our I Lan Foods and the     動，以慶祝旺旺宜蘭食品成立60周年，集
30th anniversary of the Group’s first factory in the Chinese mainland. Let’s   團在中國境內第一家工廠建廠30周年，讓
continue the tradition of Want Want and get united to win!                     我們一起傳承旺旺，大團結一定贏！

              年 報 旺旺精神
              2022/2023       Spirit of Want Want




MANAGEMENT PHILOSOPHY:
經營理念：




               People Oriented
Hot-Kid’s round head shape signifies congeniality.
The head seems to have just been washed but the
hair is not dry: this is known as “hair holding”. “Hair
holding” originated from <Zhou Gong Quotation>
and came to symbolise eagerness to recruit talents.

In our Chairman’s words, our Company is very
grateful for and cherishes our talents. Our Company
is like a big family: we should cherish the fate that
brought us together, play our part and cherish this
family, and put one another in our own hearts.

                 緣      頭圓渥髮
旺仔的頭形是圓，代表「投緣」。頭部好像剛洗
完發，發未干，故有握發之稱。「握發」出自
《周公誡子》中，「一沐三握發，一飯三吐哺，
猶恐失天下之士」。所以握發也表示有延攬賢
士的意思。

董事長的話：公司非常惜緣惜才，公司就像一
個大家庭，大家有緣在一起，應該盡我們的職
責，珍惜這個家庭，亦即你心中有我，我心中
有你，這才是真正惜緣。




                       Self Confidence                                                           Unity
Confidence comes with good preparation. Hot-Kid’s smiling           Hot-Kid’s open arms reflect our team spirit. His welcoming left
countenance reflects our belief in preparedness and the             arm denotes our unity. We believe that with the right strategies
confidence that glows with it. The heart-shaped tongue of Hot-      and concerted effort, victory is already half won. Even with less-
Kid represents our sincere heart to the Group: Be faithful to       than-expected performances, our collective efforts will ensure
the leaders, be kind to the subordinates, and be sincere to our     minimal deviations.
customers. This effort would definitely enhance our performances!


                  自信        笑口由己            誠心                                          大團結         左手擁抱
旺仔笑口常開代表充滿自信。你的自信來自你周全的準                                            旺仔的雙手展開作擁抱狀，左手表示大團結。如果策略是
備，當你充滿自信時，一定可以充滿笑容。自信是我們做                                           對的，會因團結而創造無限美好的前景，即使策略是失敗
事的第一基礎。笑口中舌如心形，代表著我們赤誠的心：                                           的，也會因團結而使損失降到最低。在一個大家庭裡，大
對待上級全心服從愛戴，對待下屬關心倍至，對待客戶誠                                           家務必心連心，手連手，團結在一起。
心誠意，必能處處逢源、事半功倍。

                                                                                                       ANNUAL REPORT


                                                                                 旺旺精神 年 報
                                                                             Spirit of Want Want      2022/2023




MANAGEMENT GOALS:
經營指標：
                                                  Elite Dragons of the World
           Hot-Kid’s open arms reflect WANT WANT is a big united family which brings together outstanding talents from all
           over the world. With the development and expansion of our businesses, the Giant Dragon of the World is born.

                                                      世界聚龍            立志雄心
           旺仔張開雙臂有招攏聚集之意。大家有緣相聚在一起，為共同的目標創造更多騰飛的龍，隨著事業的
           發展，一條條騰飛的龍匯聚在這個大家庭中，誕生為世界的「巨」龍！



                                       United with People of Common Aspirations
           Hot-Kid’s right arm denotes a helping hand. Corporate advancement is the product of the intricate interplay
           among talent, skills, capital funds and markets. As we strive to achieve our corporate vision of becoming the “Elite
           Dragons of the World”, our doors are always open to people of common aspirations and those who share our
           ideals.
                                                     結合志同道              右手提携
           旺仔右手擁抱表示提携，亦即相互幫忙提携之意。企業發展的動力，不外乎是人才、技術、資金、市
           場，我們以朝著「世界聚龍」的目標前進，尚有賴更多理念一致，志同道合的賢士，不論是技術的提
           供，亦或是共同經營，都結合在一起。


                                                High Margins, Great Success
           Hot-Kid’s upward-looking eyes point to where our management sights are set – the skies. We aim high and
           achieve far. Only through prudent management and judicious foresight will our enterprise progress with
           sustainable profits. Preparedness also prevents any unwanted events. With good yields, our shareholders and
           employees will share the fruits of our success.

                                                  高利潤         高成果          高瞻遠矚
           旺仔的眼睛往上看表示企業經營要看得遠，經營得法。利潤是企業延續的生命。經營得法，掌握該有
           的利潤在手中，必須靠大家敏銳的眼光，能夠洞察先機，防範未然，避免事後的彌補。公司一定可得
           到高的利潤，員工也定能分享其成果。



                                               Successful Business Ventures
           Hot-Kid’s roll-up sleeves and pants imply embracing our future with hard work. To realize our goal of becoming the
           “Elite Dragons of the World”, we will undertake new ventures periodically. Every employee should travel the extra
           mile and ensure tasks are successfully accomplished.

                                                  開創空前新事業                  奮袂而起
           旺仔挽起的袖手及褲子，表示呼應空前新事業，並勤奮努力。我們立志雄心成為「世界聚龍」，年年都
           將會有新的事業體，為了開創新事業，大家要在自己的工作崗位上努力勤奮地完成公司交付給大家的
           任務。



                                      Prosperity for the Group and the Individuals
           Hot-Kid’s bare feet symbolise our unwavering pragmatism. To garner customer support and recognition, a
           company needs to conduct its business honestly and earnestly. To win our customers’ and associates’ support,
           every employee should be conscientious and down-to-earth when dealing with them. Consequently, a company
           will prosper and likewise its employees.

                                                公司旺旺大家旺旺                    腳踏實地
           旺仔四平八穩沒有穿鞋的大腳，表示腳踏實地。企業要得到社會大眾的支持與肯定，只有腳踏實地，
           實實在在做事。相同的道理，每一個人都能腳踏實地做事、做人，也會受到大家的尊重與支持。如
           此，公司必然旺旺，大家也必然旺旺。

                                                                    Major Awards and Recognition     2022/2023




旺旺榮獲第七屆中國工業大獎
中國工業大獎是國務院批准設立的工業領域最高獎項，旨在表彰堅持科學發展觀、走新時代中國特色
新型工業化道路，代表中國工業化的方向、道路和精神，代表工業發展最高水平，對增強綜合國力、
推動國民經濟發展做出重大貢獻的工業企業和項目，以樹立一批優秀標桿企業和項目，並帶動形成
一大批具有核心競爭力的企業和項目。獎項由中國工業經濟聯合會聯合13家全國性工業行業聯合會
（協會）共同實施。2023年3月，旺旺「低碳節能及自動化技術的研究與應用」榮獲第七屆中國工業大獎項
目獎。

Want Want Won the 7th China Grand Awards for Industry
The China Grand Awards for Industry is the highest award in China’s industrial field approved by the
State Council. It aims to recognize industrial enterprises and projects that adhere to the scientific
development concept, follow the new industrialization road with Chinese characteristics in the new
era, represent the direction, path and spirit of industrialization in China, represent the highest level
of industrial development and make significant contributions to strengthening the comprehensive
national strength and promoting national economic development in order to establish a number of outstanding benchmark
enterprises and projects and to lead the formation of a large number of enterprises with core competitiveness. The award
was jointly presented by the China Federation of Industrial Economics and 13 national industrial federations (associations).
In March 2023, Want Want’s “Research and Application of Low Carbon, Energy Saving, and Automation Technologies”
project was honored with the Project Award at the 7th China Grand Awards for Industry.

              年 報 管理層討論及分析
               2022/2023    Management Discussion and Analysis




SUMMARY                                                                             概覽
During the 2022FY, the Group’s total revenue declined by 4.4% year-                 2022 財 年， 集 團 總 收 益 較 去 年 同 期 衰 退
on-year to RMB22,928.2 million. The revenue of the dairy products and               4.4%，達到人民幣 229.282 億元。乳品及
beverages segment recorded a mid-teen year-on-year decline in 2022FY                飲料類因疫情對聚會、部分消費場景的限
due to the pandemic restrictions on gatherings and some consumption                 制，使 2022 財年收益同比衰退中雙位數，
scenarios, bringing pressure to the overall performance of the Group’s              致集團全年整體業績表現承壓，但隨著日
annual results. However, with the gradual normalization of day-to-day               常經濟活動逐步恢復常態後，銷售活動已
economic activities, sales activities gradually returned to normal in the           於 2022 財年第四季度（2023 年 1 至 3 月）
fourth quarter of 2022FY (January to March of 2023). The revenue of rice            逐步恢復常態正常軌道，另外米果類、休
crackers and the snack foods segments (together accounted for 51% of                閒食品類（合計收益佔比 51%）同比仍保持
total revenue) maintained a mid-to-high single-digit growth year-on-year,           成長中到高個位數，其中：冰品小類和豆
with double-digit growth in each of the popsicles sub-category and beans,           類、果凍及其他小類均實現雙位數增長，

CHAIRMAN AND CHIEF EXECUTIVE OFFICER                                              主席及行政總裁
Code provision C.2.1 provides that the roles of chairman and chief executive      根據守則條文第C.2.1條，主席與行政總裁
should be separate and should not be performed by the same person.                的角色應予區分，不應由一人同時兼任。
Our Company deviates from this provision because Mr. Tsai Eng-Meng                由於蔡衍明先生擔任主席兼行政總裁兩個
performs both the roles of chairman and chief executive. Mr. Tsai is the          職務，故本公司偏離此條文。蔡先生為本
founder of our Group and has over 46 years of experience in the food and          集團之創辦人，於食品及飲料行業方面擁
beverages industry. Given the current stage of development of our Group,          有逾46年經驗。考慮到本集團目前之發展
the Board believes that vesting the two roles in the same person provides         階段，董事會認為，由一人同時兼任主席
our Company with strong and consistent leadership and facilitates the             與行政總裁，在實施並執行本集團的業務
implementation and execution of our Group’s business strategies. We shall         策略時，可為本公司提供強大而貫徹之領
nevertheless review the structure from time to time in light of the prevailing    導。然而，本集團將根據屆時情況不時檢
circumstances.                                                                    討現行架構。

one another as appropriate. Appointments to the Board will be made based          並於可行的情況下在該等因素之間取得適
on merits and the contributions that the individual is expected to bring to       當的平衡。在適當地考慮到董事會成員多
the Board, with due regard to the benefits of diversity in the Board. The         元化帶來的益處下，董事會成員的委任基

DIVIDEND POLICY                                                                       股息政策
The Company has adopted a dividend policy (the “Dividend Policy”). It is the          本公司已採納股息政策（「股息政策」）。作為
policy of the Company that, in recommending or declaring dividends, the               本公司一貫的政策，在建議或宣佈股息時，
Board shall make distributions to allow its shareholders to participate in the        本公司董事會應進行派發以允許股東分享其
Company’s profits whilst retaining adequate cash reserves for meeting its             利潤同時維持充足的現金儲備以滿足其日常
requirements for working capital and long-term sustainable development.               營運資金及長期可持續發展的要求。


The Board has the discretion to propose, declare and distribute dividends             在不違反公司章程細則及一切適用法律與
to the shareholders of the Company, subject to the Articles of Association            法規的前提下、並經考慮以下列明的因
of the Company and all applicable laws and regulations and taking into                素，董事會有酌情權向本公司股東提議、
account the factors set out below.                                                    宣佈及派發股息。


The Board shall take into account the following factors of the Group when             董事會在考慮進行宣佈及支付股息時應計
considering the declaration and payment of dividends:                                 及集團的以下因素：


(i)     the financial performance and cash flow situation of the Group;               (i)     本集團之財務表現及現金流狀況；


(ii)    the Group’s business strategies and operations, including future              (ii)    本集團之業務策略及營運，包括未來
        capital requirements and investment needs;                                            對資金需求及投資需要；


(iii)   economic conditions and other internal or external factors that may           (iii)   可能對本集團業務或財務表現及狀況
        have an impact on the business or financial performance and position                  產生影響之經濟狀況及其他內部或外
        of the Group;                                                                         部因素；


(iv)    amount of share repurchases made during the period; and                       (iv)    期內進行的股份回購的金額；及


(v)     any other factors that the Board may consider relevant.                       (v)     其他董事會認為相關的因素。


The Company in general meeting may from time to time declare dividends                本公司股東大會可不時向其成員宣佈股
to be paid to the members but no dividends shall be declared in excess of             息，惟其金額不可多於董事會建議的金
the amount recommended by the Board.                                                  額。


In addition, the Directors may from time to time pay such interim dividends           除此之外，董事可不時以其認為適宜的金
or special dividends of such amounts and on such dates as they think fit.             額及日期支付中期股息，或特別股息。


The Company may declare and pay dividends by way of cash or warrant or                在不違反公司章程細則的前提下，本公司
by other means that the Board considers appropriate, subject to its Articles          可宣佈及支付現金或認股權證或董事會認
of Association.                                                                       為適宜的其他形式的股息。

agreed to produce and sell to San Want Group in respect of certain “Water          4月1日起至2023年3月31日止為期兩年期
God (水神)” branded antiseptic and sanitising products (“Products”) (“Product        間，(i)本集團同意就若干「水神」品牌除菌
Processing and Sale Transaction”), and (ii) San Want Group agreed to sell,         及消毒產品（「產品」）進行生產並銷售給神
and the Group agreed to purchase, the Products from San Want Group for             旺集團（「產品生產及銷售交易」），及(ii)神
onward sales and distributions (“Product Purchase Transaction”) for a term         旺集團同意向本集團出售及本集團同意向
of two years from 1 April 2021 to 31 March 2023.                                   神旺集團購買產品，以供進行銷售和分銷

Renewed Property Lease Agreements                                                     已更新物業租賃協議
On 28 April 2023, certain members of the Group, as tenants, entered                   於2023年4月28日，本集團若干成員公司
into renewed property lease agreements (the “Renewed Property Lease                  （作為承租方）與相關出租方（各出租方根據
Agreements”) with the relevant landlords, each of which is a connected                上市規則的定義均是本公司的關連人士）就
person of the Company within the meaning of the Listing Rules, in respect of          重續若干位於上海及台北的物業的租賃訂
the renewal of the leases of certain properties located in Shanghai or Taipei,        立已更新物業租賃協議（「已更新物業租賃
each for a term of two years from 1 April 2023 to 31 March 2025 (both dates           協議」），每個租賃期限為期兩年，自2023
inclusive).                                                                           年4月1日起至2025年3月31日止（包括首尾
                                                                                      兩日）。


According to HKFRS 16 “Leases”, the Group, as the lessees, is required                根據香港財務報告準則第16號「租賃」，本
to recognise the leases as right-of-use assets and lease liabilities in               集團（作為承租方）須將租賃於其綜合資產
the consolidated balance sheet of the Group. As such, entering into the               負債表內確認為使用權資產和租賃負債。
Renewed Property Lease Agreements and the transactions contemplated                   因此，在上市規則下，訂立已更新物業租
thereunder would be regarded as acquisitions of assets by the Group under             賃協議及其項下擬進行之交易將被視為本
the Listing Rules. The lease transactions contemplated under the Renewed              集團收購資產，而已更新物業租賃協議項
Property Lease Agreements constitute connected transactions of the                    下擬進行的租賃交易構成本公司之關連交
Company. Further details are set out in the announcement of the Company               易。有關進一步詳情載於本公司日期為
dated 28 April 2023.                                                                  2023年4月28日之公告。

      Amendments to HKAS 16 prohibit an entity from deducting from the                     香港會計準則第16號之修訂禁止實體
      cost of an item of property, plant and equipment any proceeds from                   從出售物業、廠房及設備項目之任何
      selling items produced while bringing that asset to the location and                 所得款項中扣除使資產能夠達到以管
      condition necessary for it to be capable of operating in the manner                  理層擬定方式營運所需之位置及條件
      intended by management. Instead, an entity recognises the proceeds                   之過程中產生之成本。相反，實體須
      from selling any such items, and the cost of those items as determined               於損益中確認銷售任何有關項目之所
      by HKAS 2 Inventories , in profit or loss. The Group has applied the                 得款項及據香港會計準則第2號存貨
      amendments retrospectively to items of property, plant and equipment                 所釐定之該等項目之成本。本集團已
      made available for use on or after 1 April 2021. Since there was no                  對2021年4月1日貨之後的物業、廠房
      sale of items produced prior to the property, plant and equipment                    及設備項目追溯應用該修訂。由於在
      being available for use, the amendments did not have any impact on                   物業、廠房及設備可供使用前並無銷
      the financial position or performance of the Group.                                  售所產生的項目，故該等修訂對本集
                                                                                           團的財務狀況或表現無任何影響。

      Amendments to HKAS 37 clarify that for the purpose of assessing                      香港會計準則第37號之修訂澄清，就
      whether a contract is onerous under HKAS 37, the cost of fulfilling                  根據香港會計準則第37號評估合約是
      the contract comprises the costs that relate directly to the contract.               否屬虧損性而言，履行合約的成本包
      Costs that relate directly to a contract include both the incremental                括與合約直接相關的成本。與合約直
      costs of fulfilling that contract (e.g., direct labour and materials) and            接相關的成本包括履行該合約的增量
      an allocation of other costs that relate directly to fulfilling that contract        成本（例如直接勞工及材料）及與履行
      (e.g., an allocation of the depreciation charge for an item of property,             合約直接相關的其他成本分配（例如
      plant and equipment used in fulfilling the contract as well as contract              分配履行合約所用物業、廠房及設備
      management and supervision costs). General and administrative costs                  項目的折舊費用以及合約管理及監管
      do not relate directly to a contract and are excluded unless they are                成本）。一般及行政成本與合約並無
      explicitly chargeable to the counterparty under the contract. The Group              直接關連，除非根據合約明確向對手
      has applied the amendments prospectively to contracts for which it                   方收取，否則不包括在內。本集團已
      has not yet fulfilled all its obligations at 1 April 2022 and no onerous             對於2022年4月1日尚未履行所有義務
      contracts were identified. Therefore, the amendments did not have                    的合約採用未來適用法進行修訂，且
      any impact on the financial position or performance of the Group.                    並無識別出虧損性合約。因此，該等
                                                                                           修訂對本集團的財務狀況或表現並無
                                                                                           任何影響。

     Annual Improvements to HKFRSs 2018-2020 set out amendments to                      2018年至2020年香港財務報告準則
     HKFRS 1, HKFRS 9, Illustrative Examples accompanying HKFRS 16,                     之年度改進 載列香港財務報告準則第
     and HKAS 41. Details of the amendments that are applicable to the                  1號、香港財務報告準則第9號、香港
     Group are as follows:                                                              財務報告準則第16號隨附之說明性示
                                                                                        例及香港會計準則第41號之修訂。預
                                                                                        期將適用於本集團之該等修訂詳情如
                                                                                        下：

     •     HKFRS 9 Financial Instruments : clarifies the fees that an entity            •     香港財務報告準則第9號金融工
           includes when assessing whether the terms of a new or modified                     具： 澄清實體於評估一項新訂
           financial liability are substantially different from the terms of the              或經修訂金融負債之條款是否
           original financial liability. These fees include only those paid or                實質上不同於原金融負債之條
           received between the borrower and the lender, including fees                       款時所計入之費用。該等費用
           paid or received by either the borrower or lender on the other’s                   僅包括借款人與貸款人之間已
           behalf. The Group has applied the amendment prospectively                          付或已收之費用，其中包括借
           from 1 April 2022. As there was no modification or exchange of                     款人或貸款人代表另一方支付

2.   SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                  2.          （續）
                                                                                      重要會計政策摘要
     (continued)

     2.4   Subsidiaries                                                               2.4   附屬公司

     2.4.1 Consolidation                                                              2.4.1 合併賬目

     A subsidiary is an entity (including a structured entity) over which the         附屬公司指本集團擁有控制權的所有
     Group has control. The Group controls an entity where the Group is               主體（包括結構化主體）。當本集團因
     exposed to, or has rights to, variable returns from its involvement with         參與該主體的營運而承擔可變回報的
     the entity and has the ability to affect those returns through its power         風險或享有可變回報的權益並有能力
     over the entity. Subsidiaries are consolidated from the date on which            透過其對該主體的權力影響此等回報
     control is transferred to the Group except for business combination              時，本集團即控制該主體。附屬公司
     under common control as described below. They are deconsolidated                 在控制權轉移至本集團之日起合併
     from the date that control ceases.                                               入賬（除下述同一控制下企業合併以
                                                                                      外）。附屬公司在控制權終止之日起
                                                                                      停止合併入賬。

     Inter-company transactions, balances, income and expenses on                     集團內公司之間的交易、交易的結
     transactions between group companies are eliminated. Profits and                 餘、收入及開支予以對銷。來自集團
     losses resulting from inter-company transactions that are recognised             內公司間的利潤和損失（確認於資產）
     in assets are also eliminated unless the transaction provides evidence           亦予以對銷，除非交易提供轉讓資產
     of an impairment of the transferred asset. Accounting policies                   減值證據則另作別論。附屬公司的會
     of subsidiaries have been changed where necessary to ensure                      計政策已按需要作出改變，以確保與
     consistency with the policies adopted by the Group.                              本集團採用的政策保持一致。

     (a)   Business combinations                                                      (a)   業務合併

     (i)   Merger accounting for business combination under common                    (i)   同一控制下企業合併的合併法
           control

     The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial                  綜合財務報表中納入同一控制下企業
     statements of the combining entities or businesses in business                   合併中的合併實體或業務的財務報
     combinations under common control as if they had been combined                   表，視同該合併實體或業務自最終控
     from the date when the combining entities or businesses first came               股股東開始實施控制時一直是合併體
     under the control of the ultimate controlling shareholder.                       系。

     The net assets of the combining entities or businesses are                       合併實體或業務的資產淨值從最終控
     consolidated using the carrying amount from the ultimate controlling             股股東的角度以賬面值合併。鑒於最
     shareholder’s perspective. No amount is recognised for goodwill or the           終控股股東的利益的延續，在同一控
     excess of the Group’s interest in the book value of the net assets over          制下企業合併時，商譽或本集團對淨
     cost at the time of the common control combination, to the extent of             資產賬面值超出成本部分的權益，不
     the continuation of the ultimate controlling shareholder’s interest.             予以確認。

     Transaction costs, including professional fees, registration fees, costs         交易費用，包括專業費、註冊費、向
     of furnishing information to shareholders, costs or losses incurred              股東提供信息的費用及結合以前獨立
     in combining operations of the previously separate businesses and                的業務引起的費用或損失等，這些與
     other costs incurred in relation to the common control combination               同一控制下合併採用合併會計處理有
     that is to be accounted for by using the merger accounting method are            關的費用都要在發生當期作為費用確
     recognised as expenses in the period in which they are incurred.                 認。

     2.5   Associates                                                                    2.5   聯營公司

     An associate is an entity over which the Group has significant                      聯營公司為本集團對其有重大影響力
     influence but not control, generally accompanying a shareholding of                 而無控制權的實體，通常附帶有20%
     between 20% and 50% of the voting rights. Investments in associates                 至50%投票權的股權。聯營公司投
     are accounted for using the equity method of accounting. Under the                  資以權益法入賬。根據權益法，投資
     equity method, the investment is initially recognised at cost, and the              初步以成本確認，賬面值會增加或減
     carrying amount is increased or decreased to recognise the investor’s               少，以確認投資者佔被投資方收購日
     share of the profit or loss of the investee after the date of acquisition.          期後損益之比例。於收購於聯營公司
     Upon the acquisition of the ownership interest in an associate, any                 之擁有權權益時，聯營公司之成本與
     difference between the cost of the associate and the Group’s share of               本集團應佔聯營公司之可辨認資產及
     the net fair value of the associate’s identifiable assets and liabilities is        負債之公平淨值之任何差額入賬列作
     accounted for as goodwill.                                                          商譽。

     If the ownership interest in an associate is reduced but significant                如聯營公司的權益持有被削減但仍保
     influence is retained, only a proportionate share of the amounts                    留重大影響力，只有按比例將之前在
     previously recognised in other comprehensive income is reclassified                 其他全面收益中確認的數額重新分類
     to profit or loss where appropriate.                                                至損益（如適用）。

     2.7   Foreign currency translation                                               2.7   外幣折算

     (a)   Functional and presentation currency                                       (a)   功能和列報貨幣

     Items included in the financial statements of each of the Group’s                本集團每個主體的財務報表所列項目
     entities are measured using the currency of the primary economic                 均以該主體經營所在的主要經濟環境
     environment in which the entity operates (“the functional currency”).            的貨幣（「功能貨幣」）計量。本公司的

     The Group’s principal subsidiaries were incorporated in the PRC and              的主要附屬公司於中國註冊成立及該
     these subsidiaries considered RMB as their functional currency. Since            等附屬公司視人民幣為其功能貨幣。
     the majority of the assets and operations of the Group are located in            由於本集團大部分資產及業務位於中
     the PRC, the consolidated financial statements are presented in RMB              國，故綜合財務報表以人民幣呈報
     (unless otherwise stated) to reflect the primary economic environment            （除另有說明外）以反映本集團業務所在
     of the Group’s operations.                                                       主要經濟環境。

     (b)   Transactions and balances                                                  (b)   交易及結餘

     transactions or valuation where items are remeasured. Foreign                    算此等交易產生的匯兌收益和虧損以

     transactions and from the translation at year-end exchange rates of              終匯率折算產生的匯兌收益和虧損在
     monetary assets and liabilities denominated in foreign currencies are            綜合收益表確認。
     recognised in the consolidated income statement.

     All foreign exchange gains and losses are presented in the                       所有匯兌收益和虧損在綜合收益表內
     consolidated income statement within ‘other gains – net’.                        的「其他收益－淨額」中列報。

     Translation differences on non-monetary financial assets and liabilities         非貨幣性金融資產及負債（例如按公
     such as equities held at fair value through profit or loss are recognised        平值透過損益持有的權益）的折算差
     in profit or loss as part of the fair value gain or loss. Translation            額在損益中確認為公平值收益和虧損
     differences on non-monetary financial assets such as equities                    的一部份。非貨幣性金融資產（例如
     classified as FVOCI are included in other comprehensive income.                  分類為按公平值透過其他全面收益記

     2.8    Property, plant and equipment                                             2.8   物業、廠房及設備

      continue to be recognised in the income statement as other income                  益。本集團收取付款之權利確立時，
      when the Group’s right to receive payments is established.                         有關投資之股息繼續於收益表中確認
                                                                                         為其他收入。

     2.18 Financial liabilities                                                          2.18 金融負債

     2.18.1 Classification                                                               2.18.1 分類

     The Group’s financial liabilities include trade payables, accruals and              本集團的金融負債包括貿易應付款、
     other payables, lease liabilities, borrowings and other non-current                 應計費用及其他應付款、租賃負債、
     liabilities.                                                                        借款及其他非流動負債。

     2.18.2 Recognition and derecognition                                                2.18.2 確認及終止確認

     Financial liabilities are recognised when and only when the Group                   當且僅當本集團成為工具合約條款的
     becomes a party to the contractual provisions of the instruments and                訂約方及於交易日，金融負債予以確
     on a trade date basis. A financial liability is derecognised when and               認。當且僅當責任消除時，即相關合
     only when the liability is extinguished, that is, when the obligation               同中規定的義務被解除、取消或到期
     specified in the relevant contract is discharged, cancelled or expires.             時，金融負債才終止確認。

     2.18.3 Measurement                                                                  2.18.3 計量

     All financial liabilities are recognised initially at their fair value and          所有金融負債首次按其公平值確認，
     subsequently measured at amortised cost, using the effective interest               及其後以實際利率法按攤銷成本計
     method, unless the effect of discounting would be insignificant, in                 量，惟倘折現之影響並不重大，則按
     which case they are stated at cost                                                  成本列賬。

     Trade payables are recognised initially at fair value and subsequently              貿易應付款初始以公平值確認，其後
     measured at amortised cost using the effective interest method.                     利用實際利率法按攤銷成本計量。

     2.19 Borrowings                                                                     2.19 借款

     Borrowings are recognised initially at fair value, net of transaction               借款初始按公平值並扣除產生的交易
     costs incurred. Borrowings are subsequently stated at amortised cost;               費用確認。借款其後按攤銷成本列
     any difference between the proceeds (net of transaction costs) and the              賬；所得款（扣除交易成本）與贖回價
     redemption value is recognised in the consolidated income statement                 值的任何差額利用實際利率法於借款
     over the period of the borrowings using the effective interest method.              期間內在綜合收益表確認。

     Fees paid on the establishment of loan facilities are recognised as                 設立貸款融資時支付的費用倘部分或
     transaction costs of the loan to the extent that it is probable that some           全部融資將會很可能提取，該費用確
     or all of the facility will be drawn down. In this case, the fee is deferred        認為貸款的交易費用。在此情況下，
     until the draw-down occurs. To the extent there is no evidence that it              費用遞延至貸款提取為止。如沒有證
     is probable that some or all of the facility will be drawn down, the fee is         據證明部分或全部融資將會很可能被
     capitalised as a prepayment for liquidity services and amortised over               提取，則該費用資本化作為流動資金
     the period of the facility to which it relates.                                     服務的預付款，並按有關的融資期間
                                                                                         攤銷。

     Borrowings are classified as current liabilities unless the Group has               除非本集團可無條件將負債的結算遞
     an unconditional right to defer settlement of the liability for at least 12         延至資產負債表日後最少12個月，否則
     months after the balance sheet date.                                                借款分類為流動負債。

      2.21 Current and deferred income tax                                             2.21 當期及遞延所得稅

      countries where the Company’s subsidiaries and associates operate                的國家於資產負債表日已頒佈或實質
      and generate taxable income. Management periodically evaluates                   上已頒佈的稅務法例計算。管理層就
      positions taken in tax returns with respect to situations in which               適用稅務法例解釋所規限的情況定期
      applicable tax regulation is subject to interpretation and considers             評估報稅表的狀況，並考量稅務機關
      whether it is probable that a taxation authority will accept an uncertain        是否很有可能接受不確定的稅務處
      tax treatment. The Group measures its tax balances either based on               理。本集團借由使用最可能金額或期
      the most likely amount or the expected value, depending on which                 望值方法之一（取決於本集團預期何
      method provides a better prediction of the resolution of the uncertainty.        種方法更能預測不確定性的結果），

     the income statement in employee benefit expense, except where                   表確認為員工福利開支（已包括在資

      2.23 Provisions                                                                   2.23 準備

      Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present legal or                   當本集團因已發生的事件而產生現有
      constructive obligation as a result of past events; it is probable that an        的法律或推定義務；很可能需要有資
      outflow of resources will be required to settle the obligation; and the           源的流出以結算義務；及金額已被可
      amount has been reliably estimated. Provisions are not recognised for             靠估計，則確認準備。但不會就未來
      future operating losses.                                                          經營虧損確認準備。

      Where there are a number of similar obligations, the likelihood that an           如有多項類似義務，其需要在結算中
      outflow will be required in settlement is determined by considering the           有資源流出的可能性，則可根據義務
      class of obligations as a whole. A provision is recognised even if the            的類別整體考慮。即使在同一義務類
      likelihood of an outflow with respect to any one item included in the             別所包含的任何一個項目相關的資源
      same class of obligations may be small.                                           流出的可能性極低，仍須確認準備。

      Provisions are measured at the present value of the expenditures                  準備採用稅前利率按照預期需結算有
      expected to be required to settle the obligation using a pre-tax rate             關義務的支出現值計量，該利率反映
      that reflects current market assessments of the time value of money               當時市場對金錢時間值和有關義務固
      and the risks specific to the obligation. The increase in the provision           有風險的評估。隨著時間過去而增加
      due to passage of time is recognised as interest expense.                         的準備確認為利息費用。

      when control of the products has transferred, being when the products             產品的控制權已轉移（即產品交付予
      are delivered to the customer, the customer has full discretion over              客戶）、客戶可全權決定產品的銷售
      the channel and price to sell the products, and there is no unfulfilled           渠道及售價及並無可能影響客戶接納
      obligation that could affect the customer’s acceptance of the products.           產品的未履行責任時確認。當產品運
      Delivery occurs when the products have been shipped to the specific               送到指定地點時交付即告完成。當客
      location, the risks of obsolescence and loss have been transferred                戶按照銷售合約接納產品，或接納條
      to the customer, and either the customer has accepted the products                款已失效，或本集團有客觀證據證明
      in accordance with the sales contract, the acceptance provisions                  所有接納標準均已達成時，產品毀損

      acceptance have been satisfied.

      2.27 Leases                                                                        2.27 租賃

      Group.

      The Group leases various offices and warehouses. Rental contracts                  本集團租賃各種辦公室及倉庫。租約
      are typically made for fixed periods but may have extension options.               一般為固定期，惟可能涵蓋延期選擇
      Lease terms are negotiated on an individual basis and contain a wide               權。租賃條款按個別基準協商且包含
      range of different terms and conditions. The lease agreements do not               多種不同條款及條件。租賃協議並無
      impose any covenants, and leased assets may not be used as security                施加任何條款，而租賃資產不得用作
      for borrowing purposes.                                                            借款的擔保品。

      Each lease payment is allocated between the liability cost and finance             每筆租賃付款分配至負債成本及融資
      cost. The finance cost is charged to profit or loss over the lease period          成本。融資成本於租期內自損益扣
      so as to produce a constant periodic rate of interest on the remaining             除，藉此制定各期間負債結餘的固定
      balance of the liability for each period. The right-of-use assets are              週期利率。使用權資產按資產可使用
      depreciated over the shorter of the asset’s useful life and the lease              年期或租期（以較短者為準）以直線法
      term on a straight-line basis. If the Group is reasonably certain to               折舊。倘本集團合理確定行使購買選
      exercise a purchase option, the right-of-use asset is depreciated over             擇權，則使用權資產於相關資產的可
      the underlying asset’s useful life.                                                使用年期內予以折舊。

     2.27 Leases (continued)                                                                （續）
                                                                                      2.27 租賃

     Assets and liabilities arising from a lease are initially measured on a          租賃產生的資產及負債初步按現值計
     present value basis. Lease liabilities include the net present value of          量。租賃負債包括固定付款（包括實
     fixed payments (including in-substance fixed payments).                          質固定付款）的淨現值。

     Lease payments to be made under reasonably certain extension                     根據合理確定擴大選擇權作出的租賃
     options are also included in the measurement of the liability.                   付款亦計入負債的計量。

     The lease payments are discounted using the interest rate implicit in            租賃付款額按照租賃內含利率貼現。
     the lease. If that rate cannot be determined, the lessee’s incremental           如果無法確定該利率，則應採用承租
     borrowing rate is used, being the rate that the lessee would have to             人的增量借款利率，即承租人為在類
     pay to borrow the funds necessary to obtain an asset of similar value            似經濟環境下獲得價值相近的資產，
     in a similar economic environment with similar terms and conditions.             以類似條款及條件借入資金而必須支
                                                                                      付的利率。

     To determine the incremental borrowing rate, the Group uses recent               為釐定增量借款利率，本集團以個別
     third-party financing received by the individual lessee as a starting            承租人最近收取的第三方融資作為起
     point, adjusted to reflect changes in financing condition since third            點，並調整以反映自收取第三方融資
     party financing was received.                                                    以來的融資狀況變動。

     All land in the PRC is stated-owned and no individual land ownership             所有於中國的土地均屬國家擁有，故
     right exists. The Group acquired the right to use certain land. The              並無個人土地擁有權。本集團取得使
     premiums paid for such right are treated as prepayment for operating             用若干土地的權利。就有關權利支付
     lease and recorded at cost as right-of-use assets, which are                     的地價視為經營租賃的預付款項，並
     depreciated over the lease periods using the straight-line method.               按成本入賬為使用權資產，於租賃期
                                                                                      內以直綫法折舊。

     Right-of-use assets are measured at cost comprising the following:               使用權資產按成本計量，包括以下各
                                                                                      項：

     •    the amount of the initial measurement of lease liability, and               •     租賃負債的初步計量金額；及

     •    any lease payments made at or before the commencement date                  •     於開始日期或之前所作的任何
          less any lease incentives received.                                               租賃付款減任何已收租賃優
                                                                                            惠。

     The Group applies the short-term lease recognition exemption                     本集團對其物業及其他設備的短期租
     to its short-term leases of property and other equipment (that is                賃應用短期租賃確認豁免，有關租賃

     recognised on a straight-line basis as an expense in profit or loss.             款以直線法於損益確認為開支。

      2.28 Earnings per share                                                           2.28 每股盈利

      Basic earnings per share are calculated by dividing the profit                    每股基本盈利乃按歸屬於本公司權益
      attributable to equity holders of the Company, excluding any costs of             持有人之溢利（不包括普通股以外之
      servicing equity other than ordinary shares, by the weighted average              任何支付權益成本）除以於財政年度
      number of ordinary shares outstanding during the financial year,                  內已發行普通股之加權平均數（已就
      adjusted for bonus elements in ordinary shares issued during the year             年內已發行之普通股之紅利元素作出
      and excluding treasury shares.                                                    調整以及並不包括庫存股份）計算。

      Diluted earnings per share adjust the figures used in the determination           每股攤薄盈利調整用於釐定每股基本
      of basic earnings per share to take into account the post-tax effect of           盈利之數字以計及與潛在攤薄普通股
      interest and other financing costs associated with dilutive potential             有關之利息及其他融資成本之除所得
      ordinary shares, and the weighted average number of additional                    稅後影響，及假設所有潛在攤薄普通
      ordinary shares that would have been outstanding assuming the                     股換股，則將為額外普通股之加權平
      conversion of all dilutive potential ordinary shares.                             均數。

      2.29 Dividend distribution                                                        2.29 股息分派

      Dividend distribution to the Company’s shareholders is recognised                 分派予本公司股東的股息於股息獲本
      as a liability in the Group’s and Company’s financial statements                  公司股東或董事（如適用）批准期間在
      in the period in which the dividend is approved by the Company’s                  本集團及本公司的財務報表中確認為
      shareholders or Directors, when appropriate.                                      負債。

     3.2   Capital risk management                                                   3.2   資本風險管理

     The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to safeguard the               本集團的資本管理政策，是保障本集
     Group’s ability to continue as a going concern in order to provide              團能繼續經營，以為股東提供回報和
     returns for shareholders and benefits for other stakeholders and to             為其他利益關係者提供利益，同時維
     maintain an optimal capital structure to reduce the cost of capital.            持最佳的資本結構以減低資本成本。

     In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group may             為了維持或調整資本結構，本集團可
     adjust the amount of dividends paid to shareholders, return capital to          能會調整支付予股東的股息數額、向
     shareholders, issue new shares or sell assets to reduce debts.                  股東退還資本、發行新股或出售資產
                                                                                     以減低債務。

     Consistent with others in the industry, the Group monitors capital,             與業內其他公司一樣，本集團利用淨
     which comprises all equity components, on the basis of the net                  權益負債比率監察其資本（包括所有
     gearing ratio. This ratio is calculated as total borrowings net of cash         權益部分）。此比率按已扣除現金及
     and bank balances and long-term bank deposits divided by total equity           銀行存款及長期銀行存款的總借款除
     excluding non-controlling interests.                                            以總權益（不含非控制性權益）計算。

     The net gearing ratios at 31 March 2023 and 31 March 2022 were as               於2023年3月31日及2022年3月31
     follows:                                                                        日，淨權益負債率如下：

    events that are believed to be reasonable under the circumstances.               相信為合理之未來事件之預測）而作
                                                                                     出。

    Judgement                                                                        判斷

    In the process of applying the Group’s accounting policies,                      在應用本集團的會計政策過程中，管
    management has made the following judgement, apart from those                    理層做了以下對綜合財務報表中確認

    amounts recognised in the financial statements:                                  計者除外）：

    Deferred tax assets                                                              遞延稅項資產

    Deferred tax assets are recognised for unused tax losses and                     遞延稅項資產乃就未動用稅項虧損及
    deductible temporary differences to the extent that it is probable that          可抵扣的暫時性差異確認入賬，惟以
    taxable profit will be available against which the losses and deductible         應課稅溢利可予抵銷該等虧損及可抵
    temporary differences can be utilised. Significant management                    扣的暫時性差異的金額為限。釐定可
    judgement is required to determine the amount of deferred tax assets             予確認的遞延稅項資產的金額時，管
    that can be recognised, based upon the likely timing and level of future         理層須根據可能的時間安排、未來應
    taxable profits together with future tax planning strategies. Further            課稅溢利連同未來稅項計劃戰略作出
    details are contained in Note 22 to the financial statements.                    重大判斷。進一步詳情載於財務報表
                                                                                     附註22。

    The resulting accounting estimates will, by definition, seldom equal             的會計估計如其定義，很少會與其實
    the related actual results. The estimates and assumptions that have              際結果相同。很大機會導致下個財政
    a significant risk of causing a material adjustment to the carrying              年度的資產及負債的賬面值作出重大

    contracts that are subject to a single volume threshold. Determining             按每名客戶基準對批量回扣進行分
    whether a customer will likely be entitled to a rebate depends on the            析。釐定客戶是否可能有權獲得回扣

    reserves, before distribution of their post-tax profits of the current year.      公司法定盈餘儲備的總和超過其註冊
    A company may discontinue the contribution when the aggregate sum                 資本的50%時，公司可停止劃撥。

      Singapore or Hong Kong, holding at least 25% interest in the Chinese      司的直屬控股公司在新加坡或香港成

      on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited at a total aggregate                                       易所有限公司購回2,677,000股普通
      amount of HK$13,383,000. The shares were then fully cancelled.                                        股，總額為13,383,000港元。該等股
                                                                                                            份隨後悉數註銷。
